<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The table shows Ann's marks in two tests.

|       Test    | Marks             |
|:-----------:    |:---------------------:|
|   $1$       |   $60$ out of $80$ |
|   $2$       |   $70$ out of $100$ |

In which test did Ann do better? You **must** show your working.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The table shows Ann's marks in two tests.
<emptyline>
|       Test    | Marks             |
|:-----------:    |:---------------------:|
|   $1$       |   $60$ out of $80$ |
|   $2$       |   $70$ out of $100$ |
<emptyline>
In which test did Ann do better? You **must** show your working.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the percentage score for both the tests.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Test } 1       &= \dfrac {60}{80} \times 100 \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {60 \times 100}{80} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {15 \times 4 \times 20 \times 5}{20 \times 4} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {15 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{20} \times 5}{\cancel{20} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                     &= 15 \times 5 \\\\
                     &= 75 \% \\\\
\text{Test } 2       &= \dfrac {70}{100} \times 100 \\\\
                     &= 70 \%
\end{aligned}
$

Ann did better in Test $1$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Test $1$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which of these amounts is the cheaper? Circle your answer.

$25 \%$ of $\pounds 64$ or $20 \%$ of $\pounds 75$

Show your workings.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which of these amounts is the cheaper? Circle your answer.
<emptyline>
$25 \%$ of $\pounds 64$ or $20 \%$ of $\pounds 75$
<emptyline>
Show your workings.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {First}     &= 25 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 64 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 64 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25 \times 64} {100} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 16} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 16} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                  &= \pounds 16 \\\\
\text {Second}    &= 20 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 75 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 75 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20 \times 75} {100} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20 \times 5 \times 15} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} \times 15} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                  &= \pounds 15
\end{aligned}
$

Cheaper $= 20 \% \text{ of } \pounds 75$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \% \text{ of } \pounds 75$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {First}     &= 25 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 64 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 64 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25 \times 64} {100} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 16} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 16} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                  &= \pounds 16 \\\\
\text {Second}    &= 20 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 75 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 75 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20 \times 75} {100} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {20 \times 5 \times 15} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} \times 15} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                  &= \pounds 15
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Cheaper $= 20 \% \text{ of } \pounds 75$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $23 \%$ of $1200$.


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $23 \%$ of $1200$.
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23 \% \ \text {of} \ 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {23} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 1200} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 12 \times 100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 12 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 23 \times 12 \\\\
&= 276
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$276$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23 \% \ \text {of} \ 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {23} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 1200} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 12 \times 100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 12 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 23 \times 12 \\\\
&= 276
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jill scores $17$ out of twenty on a test. What percentage is this?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jill scores $17$ out of twenty on a test. What percentage is this?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Jill's score} &= \dfrac {17} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {17 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                    &= 17 \times 5  \\\\
                    &= 85 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $5 \%$ of $360$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $5 \%$ of $360$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5\% \text { of } 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 360} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 18 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times 18 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= 18
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

You can use part (a) to help you.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $15 \%$ of $360$.
<emptyline>
You can use part (a) to help you.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15\% \text { of } 360 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 360 \\\\
&= 3 \times \Big(\dfrac {5} {100} \times 360 \Big) \\\\
&= 3 \times 18 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The table shows whether pupils in a class walk to school.

|       | Walk to school | Do **not** walk to school |
|-------|:--------------:|:-------------------------:|
|  Boys |        $2$     |              $8$          |
| Girls |        $5$     |             $10$          |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
|       | Walk to school | Do **not** walk to school |
|-------|:--------------:|:-------------------------:|
|  Boys |        $2$     |              $8$          |
| Girls |        $5$     |             $10$          |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage of the **boys** walk to school?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of the **boys** walk to school?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of boys }      &= \dfrac {2} {2 + 8} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &=  2 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \% $

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 100} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &=  2 \times 10 \\\\
                        &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

**Question**

What percentage of the **pupils** in this class walk to school?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of the **pupils** in this class walk to school?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{\% of pupils }    &= \dfrac {2 + 5} {2 + 8 + 5 + 10} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times  25 \times 4} {25} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {25} \times 4} {\cancel {25}}  \\\\
                        &= 7 \times 4  \\\\                                                    
                        &= 28 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {25} \times 4} {\cancel {25}}  \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The table below shows the results of a survey in which $60$ boys 
were asked what colour of dress girls should wear.

|       |  Blue  |  Pink  |  Black  |
|-------|:------:|:------:|:-------:|
| Total |  $15$  |  $24$  |   $21$  |

What percentage of boys thought *girls* should wear pink?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The table below shows the results of a survey in which $60$ boys 
were asked what colour of dress girls should wear.
<emptyline>
|       |  Blue  |  Pink  |  Black  |
|-------|:------:|:------:|:-------:|
| Total |  $15$  |  $24$  |   $21$  |
<emptyline>
What percentage of boys thought *girls* should wear pink?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}   &= \dfrac {24} {24 + 15 + 21} \times 100 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {24 \times 100} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 6 \times 10 \times 10} {6 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {10} \times  {10}} {\cancel {6} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 10 \\\\
                    &= 40 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \%$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Put rings round **all** the numbers which are equal to $75 \%$ 

$\dfrac{6}{8} \quad 34 \quad \% 0.75 \quad \dfrac{75}{100} \quad \dfrac{3}{4} \quad 0.34 \quad 0.3$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Put rings round **all** the numbers which are equal to $75 \%$ 
<emptyline>
$\dfrac{6}{8} \quad 34 \quad \% 0.75 \quad \dfrac{75}{100} \quad \dfrac{3}{4} \quad 0.34 \quad 0.3$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's convert all the numbers into percentages.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{6}{8}, \quad 34,  \quad 0.75, \quad \dfrac {75} {100}, \quad \dfrac {3} {4}, \quad 0.34, \quad 0.3 \\\\
&= \dfrac{6}{8} \times 100, \quad 34 \times 100,  \quad 0.75 \times 100, \quad \dfrac {75} {100} \times 100, \quad \dfrac {3} {4} \times 100, \quad 0.34 \times 100, \quad 0.3 \times 100 \\\\
&= 0.75 \times 100 \%, \quad 3400 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 0.75 \times 100 \%, \quad 34 \%, \quad 30 \% \\\\\
&= 75 \%, \quad 3400 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 34 \%, \quad 30 \%
\end{aligned}
$

Numbers that are equal $= \dfrac{6}{8}, \quad 0.75, \quad \dfrac{75}{100}, \quad \dfrac{3}{4}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {6}{8}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.75$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {75} {100}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>

&= 0.75 \times 100 \%, \quad 3400 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 75 \%, \quad 0.75 \times 100 \%, \quad 34 \%, \quad 30 \% \\\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In her examination, Manreet got $16$ marks out of $20$. What was her mark as a percentage?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In her examination, Manreet got $16$ marks out of $20$. What was her mark as a percentage?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Manreet's score}             &= \dfrac {16} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {16 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}}   \\\\
                                    &= 16 \times 5  \\\\
                                    &= 80 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80 \%$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Find $10 \%$ of $450$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $10 \%$ of $450$.
</code></pre>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

&= \dfrac {10 \times 450} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 45 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 45
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

\end{aligned}

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23 \% \ \text{of} \ 400  \\\\
&= \dfrac {23} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 400} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {23 \times 4 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 23 \times 4 \\\\
&= 92
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $15 \%$ of $240$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $15 \%$ of $240$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \text { of } 240 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 240  \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 240} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12}{4 \times 5 \times 5}\\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times 12}{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}}\\\\
&= 3 \times 12 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\ldots \%$ of $52 = 13$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\ldots \%$ of $52 = 13$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a} {100} \times 52             &= 13 \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {13 \times 100} {52}  \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {13 \times 4 \times 25} {13 \times 4}  \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4} \times 25} {\cancel{13} \times \cancel{4}}  \\\\
a                                      &= 25
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 25$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$25$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

$15 \%$ of $\ldots = 60$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$15 \%$ of $\ldots = 60$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {15} {100} \times a             &= 60 \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{60 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{15 \times 4 \times 100}{15} \\\\
a                                      &= \dfrac{\cancel{15} \times 4 \times 100}{\cancel{15}} \\\\
a                                      &= 4 \times 100 \\\\
a                                      &= 400
\end{aligned}
$

Number $= 400$
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write $\dfrac{3}{4}$ as a percentage.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write $\dfrac{3}{4}$ as a percentage.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 25 \\\\
&= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 25 \\\\
&= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $15\%$ of $300$. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $15\%$ of $300$. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ 300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times {300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 15 \times 3 \\\\
&= 45
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which of the following has the greatest value?

- $50\%$ of $10$
- $40\%$ of $20$
- $30\%$ of $30$
- $20\%$ of $40$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which of the following has the greatest value?
<emptyline>
- $50\%$ of $10$
- $40\%$ of $20$
- $30\%$ of $30$
- $20\%$ of $40$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A}        &= 50\% \text{ of } 10 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{50}{100} \times 10 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{50 \times 10}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{5\cancel{0}\times 1 \cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0} \cancel{0}}  \\\\
                &= 5 \\\\
\text{B}        &= 40\% \text{ of } 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{40}{100} \times 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{40 \times 20}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{4\cancel{0} \times 2\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                &= 8 \\\\
\text{C}        &= 30\% \text{ of } 30 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{30}{100} \times 30 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{30 \times 30}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{3\cancel{0} \times 3\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                &= 9 \\\\
\text{D}        &= 20\% \text{ of } 40 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 40 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{20 \times 40}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2\cancel{0} \times 4\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

Biggest value $= 30 \% \text{ of } 30$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30\% \text{ of } 30$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

                &= 5 \\\\
\text{B}        &= 40\% \text{ of } 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{40}{100} \times 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{40 \times 20}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{4\cancel{0} \times 2\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
                &= 8 \\\\
\text{C}        &= 30\% \text{ of } 30 \\\\

![papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png](/assets/papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png "papers/kent-college/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png")

                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 20 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                            &= 3 \times 20 \\\\
                            &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $4\%$ of $\pounds 5300$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $4\%$ of $\pounds 5300$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 5300 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {100} \times {5300} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5300} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 53 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 53 \\\\
&= \pounds 212
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $80\%$ of $\pounds535$.

- $\pounds107$
- $\pounds372$
- $\pounds400$
- $\pounds418$
- $\pounds428$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out $80\%$ of $\pounds535$.
<emptyline>
- $\pounds107$
- $\pounds372$
- $\pounds400$
- $\pounds418$
- $\pounds428$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {80} {100} \times 535 \\\\
&= \dfrac {80 \times 535} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 5 \times 107} {20 \times 5 } \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 4 \times \cancel{5} \times 107} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{5} } \\\\
&= 4 \times 107  \\\\
&=  \pounds {428}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 428$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If $25\%$ of the cost of building a boat is for materials. Calculate the cost of the materials in a boat costing $\pounds6000$ to build.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If $25\%$ of the cost of building a boat is for materials. Calculate the cost of the materials in a boat costing $\pounds6000$ to build.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of the materials} &= 25 \% \ \text {of} \  6000 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 6000 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 6000} {100} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 60 \times 100} {100} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 60 \times \cancel {100} }{\cancel {100}} \\\\
                              &= 25 \times 60 \\\\
                              &= \pounds 1500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1500$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of the materials} &= 25 \% \ \text {of} \  6000 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 6000 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 6000} {100} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 60 \times 100} {100} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {25 \times 60 \times \cancel {100} }{\cancel {100}} \\\\
                              &= 25 \times 60 \\\\
                              &= \pounds 1500
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $5\%$ of $24$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $5\%$ of $24$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \% \text{ of } 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 24 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 24} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 4 \times 6} {4 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 4 \times 6} {\cancel 4 \times \cancel 5 \times  5} \\\\
&= \dfrac  {6}{5} \\\\
&= 1 \dfrac  {1}{5}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \dfrac  {1}{5}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $60\%$ of $10\%$ of $900?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $60\%$ of $10\%$ of $900?$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 60 \% \ \text {of} \ 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60} {100} \times \dfrac {10} {100} \times 900 \\\\
&= \dfrac {60 \times 10 \times 900} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 9 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0} \times 1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 6 \times 9 \\\\
&= 54
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $6\%$ of six plus $8\%$ of eight. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $6\%$ of six plus $8\%$ of eight. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \% \text{ of } 6 + 8 \% \text{ of } 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6} {100} \times 6 + \dfrac{8} {100} \times 8  \\\\
&= \dfrac{6 \times 6} {100} + \dfrac{8 \times 8} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36} {100} + \dfrac{64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{36 + 64} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{100} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {100}} {\cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 1
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

An iPad used to cost $\pounds 330$. Since Brexit the price has increased by $22\%$.

How much does it cost now? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">An iPad used to cost $\pounds 330$. Since Brexit the price has increased by $22\%$.
<emptyline>
How much does it cost now? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the cost of iPad increased by $22 \%$, then the new cost will be $100 + 22 = 122 \%$ of the previous cost. 

Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New cost of iPad}              &= \dfrac {122} {100} \times 330 \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {122 \times 330} {100} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {122 \times 33\cancel{0}} {10\cancel{0}} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac{122 \times 33}{10} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac{4026}{10} \\\\
                                      &= \pounds 402.60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 402.60$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $7\%$ of seven plus $9\%$ of nine. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $7\%$ of seven plus $9\%$ of nine. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 7 \% \ \text{of} \ 7 + 9 \% \ \text{of} \ 9 \\\\
&= 7 \% \times 7 + 9 \% \times 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac{7 \times 7}{100} + \dfrac{9 \times 9}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{49}{100} + \dfrac{81}{100} \\\\  
&= \dfrac{49 + 81}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{130}{100} \\\\
&= 1.3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1.3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 60?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 60?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 6 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Use your answer to write down.

$3\dfrac{1}{3}\%$ of $\pounds 60$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Use your answer to write down.
<emptyline>
$3\dfrac{1}{3}\%$ of $\pounds 60$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 60 \\\\
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \% \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {3} \% \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {3 \times 100} \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \bigg ( \dfrac {10} {100} \times 60 \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {3}  \\\\
&= \pounds 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2$

&= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 60 \\\\

&= \dfrac {10} {3} \% \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {3 \times 100} \times 60 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \bigg ( \dfrac {10} {100} \times 60 \bigg) \\\\

**Question**

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 400?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 400?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 400} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 4 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 4 \\\\
&= \pounds 40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Question**

Use your answer to write down $7\dfrac{1}{2}\%$ of $\pounds 400.$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Use your answer to write down $7\dfrac{1}{2}\%$ of $\pounds 400.$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%

%empty%
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 7 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {2} \% \times 400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 + 5} {2} \% \times400 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (10 \% \times 400 + 5 \% \times 400) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (40 + 20) \\\\
&= \dfrac {60} {2}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 2} {2}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}}  \\\\
&= \pounds 30
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Add together $25\%$ of $20$, $\dfrac{1}{5}$ of $30$ and $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of $18$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Add together $25\%$ of $20$, $\dfrac{1}{5}$ of $30$ and $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of $18$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 25\% \ \text{of} \ 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \times 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \times 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 20} {100} + \dfrac{30} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 18} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 5} {25 \times 4} + \dfrac{5 \times 6} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times 6} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 5} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} + \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 6} {\cancel{5}} + \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3} \times 6} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 5 + 6 + 12 \\\\
&= 23
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$23$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 25\% \ \text{of} \ 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \ \text{of} \ 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {100} \times 20 + \dfrac{1} {5} \times 30 + \dfrac{2} {3} \times 18 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 20} {100} + \dfrac{30} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 18} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 4 \times 5} {25 \times 4} + \dfrac{5 \times 6} {5} + \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times 6} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4} \times 5} {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} + \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 6} {\cancel{5}} + \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3} \times 6} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 5 + 6 + 12 \\\\
&= 23
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

 In a survey $30\%$ of people were left handed only, $10\%$ were
both left and right handed. What percentage were right handed only?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex"> In a survey $30\%$ of people were left handed only, $10\%$ were
both left and right handed. What percentage were right handed only?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {\% Right handed only}    &= 100 - 30 - 10 \\\\
                                &= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \%$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $50\%$ of $80$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $50\%$ of $80$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 50 \% \text { of } 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50}{100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 80 }{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \cancel {0} \times 8 \cancel{0} } {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 8 \\\\
&= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find  $35\%$ of $80 \ \text{kg}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find  $35\%$ of $80 \ \text{kg}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35\% \ \text{of}\ 80 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 80} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 5 \times 20 \times 4}{5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 20 \times 4}{\cancel 5 \times \cancel 20} \\\\
&= 7 \times 4 \\\\
&= 28 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage of the large grid on the left is shaded?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of the large grid on the left is shaded?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total squares}        &= 10 \times 10 \\\\
                            &= 100 \\\\
\text{Shaded squares}       &= 75 \\\\
\text{\% Shaded}            &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 100 \\\\
                            &= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What fraction of the small grid on the right is shaded? Give your answer in its simplest form. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of the small grid on the right is shaded? Give your answer in its simplest form. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction Shaded}  &= \dfrac {\text{Shaded squares}} {\text{Total squares}} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {9} {24} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3 \times 3} {8 \times 3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 8} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {3} {8}$

</div>
</div>

                        &=  \dfrac {9} {24} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3 \times 3} {8 \times 3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 8} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac {3} {8}

$

**Question**

What percentage of grid(C) is shaded?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of grid(C) is shaded?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total squares}        &= 10 \times 10 \\\\
                            &= 100 \\\\
\text{Shadeded squares}     &= 28 \\\\
\text{\% Shaded}            &= \dfrac {28} {100} \times 100 \\\\
                            &= 28 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \%$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What fraction of grid(D) is shaded? Give your answer in 
its simplest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of grid(D) is shaded? Give your answer in 
its simplest form.
</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}

                        &= \dfrac {30} {100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \cancel{0}} {10 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3} {10}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

                        &= \dfrac {30} {100} \\\\

                        &= \dfrac {3} {10}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Emily scores $30$ out of $60$ in a test.

Hebe scores $60\%$ in the same test.

How many more marks did Hebe get than Emily?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Emily scores $30$ out of $60$ in a test.
<emptyline>
Hebe scores $60\%$ in the same test.
<emptyline>
How many more marks did Hebe get than Emily?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}            &= \text{Hebe's marks} - \text{Emily's marks} \\\\
                              &= 60\% \ \text{of} \ 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 60 - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {60 \times 60} {100} - 30 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {6 \cancel{0} \times 6 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} -30 \\\\
                              &= 36 - 30 \\\\
                              &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What number is $40\%$ of $75 \text{?}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What number is $40\%$ of $75 \text{?}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 40\% \ \text{of} \ 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40} {100} \times 75 \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 75} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 10 \times 3 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{4} \times 10 \times 3 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{4} \times \cancel{25}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 3 \\\\
&= 30
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {40 \times 75} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 10 \times 3 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{4} \times 10 \times 3 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{4} \times \cancel{25}} \\\\
&= 10 \times 3 \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $11\%$ of $32600$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate $11\%$ of $32600$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 11 \% \ \text {of} \ 32600 \\\\
&= \dfrac{11}{100} \times 32600 \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 32600}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{11 \times 326 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 11 \times 326 \\\\
&= 3586
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage of the grid below is shaded? You can assume all boxes are identical in size.

![papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png "papers/st-pauls-girls-school/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q08-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of the grid below is shaded? You can assume all boxes are identical in size.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total  boxes}                 &= 5 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 25 \\\\
\text{Shaded boxes}                 &= 17 \\\\
\text{\% shaded grid}               &= \dfrac {17}{25} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 4 \times 25} {25} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {17 \times 4 \times \cancel {25}} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                                    &= 17 \times 4 \\\\
                                    &= 68 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$68 \%$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $35 \%$ of $160$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $35 \%$ of $160$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 35 \% \ \text{of} \ 160 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35} {100} \times 160 \\\\
&= \dfrac {35 \times 160} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
&= 56
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$56$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $30 \%$ of $120$

- $30$
- $3.6$
- $36$ 
- $40$ 
- $64$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $30 \%$ of $120$
<emptyline>
- $30$
- $3.6$
- $36$ 
- $40$ 
- $64$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \ \text{of} \ 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac{30}{100} \times 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac{30 \times 120}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \cancel{0} \times 12 \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 12  \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
