<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write three-fifths as a percentage.

- $6 \%$
- $30 \%$
- $35 \%$
- $3 \%$
- $60 \%$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write three-fifths as a percentage.
<emptyline>
- $6 \%$
- $30 \%$
- $35 \%$
- $3 \%$
- $60 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{3}{5} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 100}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times 5}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 20 \\\\
&= 60 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the difference between $20 \%$ of $90$ and $90 \%$ of $20 \text{?}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the difference between $20 \%$ of $90$ and $90 \%$ of $20 \text{?}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 90 - 90 \% \ \text {of} \ 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 90 - \dfrac {90} {100} \times 20 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 90} {100} - \dfrac {90 \times 20} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \cancel{0} \times 9 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} - \dfrac {9 \cancel{0} \times 2 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 2  \times 9  - 9 \times 2  \\\\
&= 18 - 18 \\\\
&= 0
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What **percentage** of sweets in this bag are red?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What **percentage** of sweets in this bag are red?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {\% red sweets}               &= \dfrac {4} {16 + 4} \ \times \ 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times 20 \times 5} {20} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {20} \times 5} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                                    &= 4 \times 5 \\\\
                                    &= 20 \%   
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \%$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Phil now eats $4$ of the blue sweets.

What fraction of the sweets in the bag are now blue?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Phil now eats $4$ of the blue sweets.
<emptyline>
What fraction of the sweets in the bag are now blue?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction}                 &= \dfrac {\text{Blue sweets}} {\text{Total sweets left}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 - 4} {20 - 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 3} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 3} {\cancel {4} \times 4} \\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage of the shape below is shaded?

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q12-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q12-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q12-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of the shape below is shaded?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Shaded triangles}    &= 15 \\\\
\text {Total triangles}     &= 20 \\\\
\text {\% shaded}           &= \dfrac {\text {Shaded triangles}} {\text {Total triangles}} \times 100 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {15} {20} \times 100 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times 20} {20} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {15 \times 5 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
                            &= 15 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75 \%$

</div>
</div>

What is $30 \%$ of $550 \ \text{cm}^3?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which is bigger: $20\%$ of $25$ or $25\%$ of $20?$

(Marks will only be given for answers accompanied by appropriate working)

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which is bigger: $20\%$ of $25$ or $25\%$ of $20?$
<emptyline>
(Marks will only be given for answers accompanied by appropriate working)
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First}    &= 20\% \ \text{of} \ 25 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 25 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{20 \times 25}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{5 \times 4 \times 25}{4 \times 25}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{5 \times \cancel {4} \times \cancel {25}}{\cancel {4} \times \cancel {25}}  \\\\
                &= 5 \\\\
\text{Second}   &= 25\% \ \text{of} \ 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{25}{100} \times 20 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{25 \times 20}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{25 \times 4 \times 5}{25 \times 4}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{\cancel {25} \times \cancel {4} \times 5}{\cancel {25} \times \cancel{4}}  \\\\
                &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

Both are equal.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Both are equal.

</div>
</div>

                &= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 25 \\\\
                &= \dfrac{20 \times 25}{100}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{5 \times 4 \times 25}{4 \times 25}  \\\\
                &= \dfrac{5 \times \cancel {4} \times \cancel {25}}{\cancel {4} \times \cancel {25}}  \\\\
                &= 5 \\\\

                &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Both are equal.

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

$
\begin{aligned}

&= \dfrac {50} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {50 \times 80}{100}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \cancel {0} \times 8 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}}  \\\\
&= 5 \times 8 \\\\
&= 40
\end{aligned}
$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1\% \ \text{of} \ 80 \\\\
&= 1\% \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {5} \times \dfrac {5} {100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {5} \times 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\dfrac{4}{5}$
</code></pre>
</div>

</li>
</ul>
